> **PRJ402141 (Plum Island, Ryan) — the drawing BOM "wouldn't extract" / "moved from line to line."** Live Firestore/debug-log diagnosis (no assumptions — Jon-gated):
> - **NOT page-typing, NOT concurrency (open-lock prevents it).** ROOT CAUSE (Coach-verified): an 18-page add fires 1 PDF + 18 Storage uploads that saturate the uplink WHILE ~7 PanelCard background `_noBumpWrite` full-doc autosaves + the extraction saves pile up on the single client Firestore WriteStream → `resource-exhausted: Write stream exhausted` → the extraction save's error was **swallowed to `console.warn`** → UI showed "✓ N items" while Firestore got nothing → reload = 0 items/no report. > ## ▶ RESUME HERE (2026-07-31) — B070 catalog-PN corruption FIXED + shipped to prod v1.24.62.
> **This session flipped last night's IP66/1200 diagnosis.** The AI model extracted the parts CORRECTLY (pdf-native: `640014405`, `8660025`); ARC's `resolveInternalPartNumbers` was silently OVERWRITING correct all-digit Rittal catalog #s with description spec tokens (`640014405→IP66`, `8660025→1200`), moving the real value to `customerPartNumber`. Root cause: `_INTERNAL_PN_RE`'s `^\d{7,12}$` alternative collides with legit all-digit catalog numbers → on an all-digit BOM the >50% trigger fires BOM-wide. **Fix:** restrict `_INTERNAL_PN_RE` to dashed customer-codes only + reject IP-ratings in `_looksLikeMfrPn`. Coach APPROVE-WITH-NITS → Test V.071 → **Jon verified live** (Ref#7→`640014405`, Ref#44→`8660025`, zero corruptions, `internalPnResolutions` empty) → cherry-picked `fa1b2d53` (+ nit `8750c476`) → **prod v1.24.62 `b31ae57a`**. `recoverMisread` branch dropped (misaimed — model never misread). Doc: `docs/B070-internal-pn-catalog-corruption.md`.
> **⏳ NEXT:**
> - **B070 backfill (follow-up):** existing all-digit-catalog projects likely already corrupted; correct value survives in `customerPartNumber` → scan+restore feasible. Scope pending Jon go.
> - **bcFuzzy Fix 1** (`claude/bcfuzzy-crossfield-fix` `f10dc67f`, Coach APPROVE-WITH-NITS; fold NIT-1 = raise 4b gate to `stripped.length>=5`) — cross-field `contains` step so Vendor_Item_No parts (`800F-34RE100`) match. Ready for its own Test pass.

Services live in `serviceCards`, not `panels`, so `sp===null` → the **simplified branch** (~35773–35811) renders header+service rows+PROJECT TOTAL but **no action buttons** (Print/Send Quote, Tech Review, Send BOM for Approval, PO Received/LOST all live in the `sp`-only "third pane" block ~36084+). **Fix (Jon-approved):** lift the QUOTE SUMMARY + action block so it renders for **any** quote line (`panels.length>0 || serviceCards.length>0`); keep **Send-BOM-for-Approval hidden** for services-only (no BOM — `generateTravelerBomPdf` returns null when `!panels.length`); **skip ship-date** for services-only (Jon's call). Paneled quotes unchanged (purely additive). **STATUS: 🔨 Marc lane building** (worktree → branch `b033-services-quote-pane` → Coach review → Jon deploy). Tracked: **B033** (TODO.md).

> ## 🚨 B034 (2026-07-13) — ASAP: editing a SENT quote doesn't bump Qv.## — Jon's authoritative lock-centric spec
> Jon (ASAP): rev isn't bumping on quote changes. ROOT (Jon's diagnosis): a modal says "rev bumps on next **save**" but users never click save — saving is implicit (on change + send) → phantom-save gate never fires. **Authoritative spec (supersedes C149's print-anchored model):** (1) SENT quote = **locked** → rev **frozen**; (2) unlock → no change → relock = rev **unchanged**; (3) unlock → **ANY change → rev bumps IMMEDIATELY** + quote → **"active"** until re-sent; (4) first change after unlocking a quote with send history → **warning modal** "This Quote has been sent to the customer. Making changes will cause the Customer copy and internal copy to be different. Continue?" (continue/cancel). **★ Bundle with F005** (Print-Only in the locked overlay). **F005 scope DONE (Coach):** locked overlay = the post-send soft-block banner in `PanelListView` @:36614; print-stamp sites @:38243 (Path A) + :37068 (Path B); Print-Only = normal print + a `printOnly` flag that SKIPS the `quoteRevAtPrint` stamp (so it can't reset the cycle) via a new `arc-print-only` window event; **build B034+F005 ONE branch, B034 first**. **★★ Coach authoritative scope DONE:** ROOT = print-anchored bump cap + send never stamps `quoteRevAtPrint`. Fix (one branch, B034+F005): re-anchor caps on `max(quoteRevAtPrint,quoteSentRev)`; diverged sent-quote → **In Process** (Jon's choice) + unlock; reword warning modal to Jon's text; delete dead unlock modal; F005 Print-Only = no-bump PDF in the locked banner. Rules 1/2 already met; Rule 3 = the fix. No schema bump. Money-path → Coach re-review + live-test before deploy. **✅ Marc BUILT** → `b034-f005-quote-revision` (`4df3fd74`, +86/−37, validate PASS): all 6 B034 changes + F005 Print-Only; never-sent cadence byte-identical (guard confirmed); diverged→In Process (explicit return); Print-Only skips all 3 stamps + no unlock + reuses quote #. **✅ Coach APPROVE WITH NITS (money-path bar):** core logic CONFIRMED — never-sent byte-identical, bump-exactly-once, send-save can't self-bump, Change C unlock divergence+sent-gated, dead modal clean, data-retention PASS. **1 REQUIRED fix (F1):** Print-Only's PRE-print saves (populate @`:38507` + budgetary flip @`:38535`) aren't print-only-aware → narrow case could bump+unlock a sent quote. **1 fix (F2):** unsent-pill OR-in lights on every never-sent quote → gate on `quoteSentAt`. Filed **B036** (pre-existing: `quoteSent*` not in saveProject preserve-guards, now load-bearing). **✅ Marc applied F1+F2** → tip `3f15678a`: F1 gates all 3 pre-print persists on `!printOnly` (populate/budgetary-flip/quote#-assign; in-memory populate retained so PDF still renders; checklist eqs/sync persists confirmed non-bumping — not in hash); F2 gates the pill send-clause on `quoteSentAt` at both sites (never-sent reverts to pre-branch behavior). **✅ Coach delta APPROVE** (`3f15678a`): F1 closes the print-only bump/unlock hole end-to-end (normal print byte-unchanged); F2 correct both sites. **✅✅ DEPLOY-READY — HELD for Jon deploy + live-test cycle** (send→edit bumps once→In Process→re-send; Print-Only = no bump/no unlock). Tracked: **B034** + **F005**.

> ## 📋 B034 prior scope (C149 — SUPERSEDED by the ASAP spec above)
> Jon: post-send edits don't bump the revision → the live quote can silently diverge from the customer's copy at the same Qv.##. **Coach scoped (C149):** ROOT = bump gate + "unsent revision" pill anchored to last PRINT (`quoteRevAtPrint`); SEND (`quoteSentRev`) never participates. Bump logic already centralized in 2 save choke points (`saveProject`/`saveProjectPanel`, hash-driven, capped once per print cycle) — send just never advances the cap. **Fix (Coach Option A):** factor `_lastDeliveredRev=max(quoteRevAtPrint,quoteSentRev)` → route both bump gates + 2 pills + the warn through it; no new field, minor bump. **Jon chose: "first change after send bumps"** (once per revision, not per-keystroke — rejected the literal "every change" which recreates the Rev-293 runaway; keeps PRINT as a reset too). **STATUS: build QUEUED behind B033 deploy** (B034 edits the unsent-pill @:36088, inside B033's refactored quote-summary region → build on post-B033 master to avoid collision). > ## STEP-2 RECONCILE RE-RUN = CLEAN (2026-07-29, Jon go). Root-caused the first-run 52 collisions.
> The 52 were NOT "BC read-lag" (earlier theory) -- ROOT CAUSE = a stray NUL byte in the BOM-walk group-key separator (bcNo +   + vendorNo) while the RFQ-merge key used a space. Same item+vendor split into two non-matching group keys -> two groups -> concurrent double-write -> EntityWithSameKeyExists / etag-409. Coach could not see it (NUL renders as a space in source). Replaced the NUL with a space; group count deduped 1104 -> 887, confirming the merge. Plus N1 (filter $0.71 before ranking so junk can't mask a lower real tier) + pre-pass buildIndex mode (RFQ index built once, write-slices read cache) + project provenance in failures. All committed (6b91a491). Re-run result: failed 52 -> 1, matched 770, updated 10, conflicts 51 (down from 80), lowSourceOnly 22, keptBc 26, junkNoBc 7, bySource 245 supplier(RFQ)/8 manual/527 bc. ONE leftover = 1002 in PRJ402119 (non-MTX ambiguous Hubbell item, vendor V00374 -> needs Jon manual BC-item pick). ARC<->BC PRICE RECONCILE DONE + CLEAN; rollback audit per slice in bcPpReconcileRuns. Held-not-priced (correct, for review): 51 conflicts + 22 low-source + 1002.
